BUSINESS REVIEW                                                                業務回顧

As a leading brand of TCM healthcare service in China, “Gushengtang           「固 生 堂」作 為 中 國 一 個 中 醫 醫 療 健 康 服 務
( 固 生 堂 )” is dedicated to providing patients and customers with a             的領先品牌 ， 致力於通過線下醫療機構及
comprehensive range of TCM healthcare services and products through            線上醫療健康平台之廣泛網絡 ， 為患者和
its extensive network of offline medical institutions and online healthcare    客戶提供全面的中醫醫療健康服務及產品 。
platforms. During the Reporting Period, the Group reinforced its focus         於報告期內 ， 本集團強化基礎醫療之重心 ，
on primary care and remained steadfast in its core value of serving            堅 定 不 移 地 以「良 心 醫 ， 放 心 藥」的 核 心 價
customers with “conscientious physicians, reliable pharmaceuticals ( 良         值觀服務客戶 。
心 醫 , 放 心 藥 )”.


The comprehensive healthcare solutions of the Group that cover the             本集團貫穿疾病診療全過程和日常健康管
entire disease course and daily health management have the following           理的全面醫療健康解決方案具有以下主要
key characteristics:                                                           特點：


Proprietary supply chain with selected quality TCM                             自有供應鏈嚴選好藥材
medicinal materials

The Group has always regarded the quality of TCM medicinal materials           本集團始終將中藥材品質視為企業發展的
as the core lifeline of its business development, and continues to deepen      核心生命線 ， 持續深化自有供應鏈體系建
the building of its proprietary supply chain system to control the quality     設 ， 從源頭把控藥材品質 。 本集團在全國
of medicinal materials at source. The Group has established a stable           核心道地產區建立了穩定的藥材採購網絡 ，
medicinal material procurement network in core authentic production            與優質種植基地及供應商形成長期戰略合
areas nationwide, and formed long-term strategic partnerships with             作關係 ， 確保每一味中藥材均來自最佳產
quality planting bases and suppliers, ensuring that every TCM medicinal        區 、 遵循最佳採收時節 。
material comes from the optimal production areas and follows the

In terms of quality control, the Group has established a standardized          在 質 量 管 控 方 面 ， 本 集 團 建 立 了 覆 蓋「採 購

Pharmacopoeia (《中 國 藥 典》) to screen key indicators such as origin              殘重金屬檢測 、 有效成分含量等關鍵指標
traceability, pesticide residue and heavy metal testing, and active            進行層層把關 ， 堅決杜絕劣質藥材流入各
ingredient content of TCM medicinal materials at every stage, resolutely       環節 。
preventing inferior medicinal materials from entering any stage of the
process.

Balancing frontline clinical practice and academic                            一線臨床與學術共享並重
exchange

The Group firmly believes that the inheritance and innovation of              本集團始終堅信 ， 中醫藥的傳承與創新離
TCM cannot be achieved without the dual drivers of solid frontline            不開扎實的一線臨床實踐與活躍的學術交
clinical practice and vibrant academic exchange. While deepening its          流雙輪驅動 。 本集團在深耕一線中醫診療
commitment to frontline TCM diagnosis and treatment services, the             服務的同時 ， 積極搭建高水平學術交流平
Group actively builds high-level academic exchange platforms, striving        台 ， 致力於成為中醫藥學術繁榮的推動者
to be a promoter and leader in the prosperity of TCM academia.                與引領者 。

of offline clinics and online internet-based diagnosis and treatment          線下門診網絡及線上互聯網診療平台 ， 每

accumulating vast amounts of authentic TCM clinical data and practical        實的中醫臨床數據與診療經驗 。 本集團旗
experience. The Group’s physician team has long been rooted in the            下醫生團隊長期扎根臨床一線 ， 在中醫內
frontline of clinical practice, developing distinctive diagnostic and         科 、 婦科 、 兒科 、 皮膚科 、 腫瘤調理等多
therapeutic expertise and achieving significant clinical efficacy across      個專科領域形成了鮮明的診療特色與顯著

pediatrics, dermatology, and oncology regulation. These extensive             個人的學術成長提供了沃土 ， 也為中醫藥
frontline practices not only provide fertile ground for the academic          經驗的傳承 、 驗方的整理及療效的循證研

     market. These stores not only effectively expand our user reach                流量池 ， 還可將部分用戶轉化為深度
     and build a wider customer traffic pool, but also serve to convert             診療客戶 ， 反哺本集團旗下名醫模式
     a portion of these users into patients seeking for in-depth medical            的 門 診 業 務 ， 實 現「消 費 引 流 、 醫 療
     service. This creates a virtuous cycle of “consumer acquisition and            轉 化」的 良 性 閉 環 ， 推 動 兩 大 業 務 模
     medical service conversion”, directly empowering the renowned                  式協同發展 。
     TCM specialists business under the Group. Ultimately, this strategy
     drives the synergistic development of both business models.

  bearing bank borrowings, and convertible bonds balance of total equity      可 換 股 債 券 結 餘）除 以 權 益 總 額 再 乘 以
  and multiplied by 100%. The gearing ratio of our Group increased            100% 計 算 。 本 集 團 的 槓 桿 比 率 由 截 至2025

  primarily due to the issue of the convertible bonds. However, we have       30日 的54.6% ， 主 要 是 由 於 發 行 可 換 股 債 券

  operations. The Group has no foreign currency hedging policy. However,      本集團並無外匯對沖政策 。 然而 ， 其管理
  its management monitors foreign exchange exposures and will consider        層會監控外匯風險 ， 並將在未來有需要時

  COMPLIANCE WITH THE CORPORATE                                              遵守企業管治守則
  GOVERNANCE CODE

  The Group is committed to maintaining high standards of corporate          本集團致力維持高水平的企業管治 ， 以維
  governance to safeguard the interests of the Shareholders and to           護股東的權益並改善企業價值及問責性 。
  enhance corporate value and accountability. The Company has adopted        本 公 司 已 採 納 上 市 規 則 附 錄 C1所 載 企 業 管
  the CG Code as set out in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules as its own      治守則作為其自身的企業管治守則 。
  code of corporate governance.


  The Board considered that the Company has complied with all                董 事 會 認 為 ， 本 公 司 於 截 至2026年6月30日
  applicable code provisions as set out in the CG Code during the six        止六個月已遵守企業管治守則所載的所有
  months ended June 30, 2026, save and except for code provision C.2.1       適用守則條文 ， 惟企業管治守則第二部分
  in part 2 of the CG Code.                                                  守 則 條 文 第 C.2.1條 除 外 。


  Pursuant to code provision C.2.1 in part 2 of the CG Code, the roles       根 據 企 業 管 治 守 則 第 二 部 分 守 則 條 文 第 C.2.1
  of chairman and chief executive officer should be separate and should      條 ， 主席及首席執行官的角色應有區分 ，
  not be performed by the same individual. Mr. Tu is currently serving       不應由一人同時兼任 。 涂先生現任主席兼
  as the Chairman as well as the chief executive officer of the Company.     本公司首席執行官 。 由於涂先生為本集團
  As Mr. Tu is the founder of the Group and has been managing the            的創始人 ， 自本集團成立起一直管理本集
  Group’s business and overall strategic planning since its establishment,   團的業務及整體戰略規劃 ， 故董事認為 ，
 the Directors consider that vesting the roles of chairman and chief         將主席及首席執行官職位授予涂先生 ， 有
 executive officer in Mr. Tu is beneficial to the business prospects and     利於本集團的業務前景及管理 ， 確保本集
 management of the Group by ensuring consistent leadership within the        團內的一貫領導 。 因此 ， 董事會認為 ， 在
 Group. The Board therefore considers it is appropriate to deviate from      有關情況下偏離企業管治守則第二部分守
 code provision C.2.1 in part 2 of the CG Code in such circumstances.        則 條 文 第 C.2.1條 屬 恰 當 之 舉 。 如 有 需 要 ，
 The Board will continue to review and consider splitting the roles          經考慮本集團的整體情況 ， 董事會將繼續
 of Chairman and the chief executive officer of the Company at an            檢討並於適當時候考慮分拆主席及本公司
 appropriate time if necessary, taking into account the circumstances        首席執行官的角色 。 儘管有上述情況 ， 董
 of the Group as a whole. Notwithstanding the foregoing, the Board           事會認為管理架構對本集團營運有效 ， 且
 considers that the management structure is effective for the operation of   已經建立足夠的制衡機制 。
 the Group and those adequate checks and balances have been put in
 place.


 The Company will continue to review and monitor its corporate               本公司將繼續審閱及監查其企業管治常規
 governance practices to ensure compliance with the CG Code.                 以確保符合企業管治守則 。

1   BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                 1   編製基準

    The interim condensed consolidated financial information for             截 至2026年6月30日 止 六 個 月 的 中 期 簡
    the six months ended June 30, 2026 has been prepared in                  明綜合財務資料乃根據香港會計準則
    accordance with HKAS 34 Interim Financial Reporting. The interim         第34號 中 期 財 務 報 告 編 製 。 中 期 簡 明
    condensed consolidated financial information does not include all        綜合財務資料並未包括年度財務報表
    the information and disclosures required in the annual financial         所需的所有資料及披露 ， 並須與本集
    statements, and should be read in conjunction with the Group’s           團 截 至2025年12月31日 止 年 度 的 年 度
    annual consolidated financial statements for the year ended              綜合財務報表一併閱讀 。
    December 31, 2025.

    AND DISCLOSURES

    with those applied in the preparation of the Group’s annual              月31日 止 年 度 的 年 度 綜 合 財 務 報 表 所
    consolidated financial statements for the year ended December 31,        採用者一致 ， 惟就本期間的財務資料
    2025, except for the adoption of the following amended HKFRS             首次採用之下列經修訂香港財務報告

    financial information.

  2   CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                           2   會計政策及披露的變更
      AND DISCLOSURES (CONTINUED)                                                  （續）

      The nature and impact of the amended HKFRS Accounting                        經修訂香港財務報告準則會計準則的
      Standards are described below:                                               性質及影響載列如下：


      (a)   Amendments to HKFRS9 and HKFRS 7 Amendments to the                     (a)   香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第9號 及 香 港
            Classification and Measurement of Financial Instruments                      財 務 報 告 準 則 第7號（修 訂 本）金
            clarify that a financial asset is derecognised when the entity’s             融工具分類及計量之修訂 澄清當
            rights to the contractual cash flows expire or are transferred,              實體收取合約現金流量的權利屆

            date. The amendments introduce an accounting policy option                   終止確認；而金融負債則於結算
            to derecognise a financial liability that is settled through an              日期予以終止確認 。 該等修訂本
            electronic payment system before the settlement date if                      引入會計政策選擇 ， 即在滿足特
            specified criteria are met. The amendments clarify how to                    定條件的情況下 ， 終止確認於結
            assess the contractual cash flow characteristics of financial                算日期前通過電子支付系統結算
            assets with environmental, social and governance and other                   的金融負債 。 該等修訂本闡明如
            similar contingent features. Moreover, the amendments                        何評估具有環境 、 社會及管治以
            clarify the requirements for classifying financial assets with               及其他類似或有特徵的金融資產
            non-recourse features and contractually linked instruments.                  的合約現金流量特徵。 此外，
            The amendments also include additional disclosures                           該等修訂本闡明對具有無追索權
            for investments in equity instruments designated at fair                     特徵的金融資產及合約掛鈎工具
            value through other comprehensive income and financial                       的分類要求 。 該等修訂本亦包括
            instruments with contingent features. Since the Group’s                      對指定為按公允價值計入其他全
            accounting policy for the derecognition of financial assets                  面收益的權益工具投資及具有或
            and liabilities in prior years aligned with the amendments                   有特徵的金融工具的額外披露要
            and the Group did not have the financial assets that were                    求 。 鑒於本集團過往年度有關金
            addressed by the amendments, the amendments did not                          融資產及負債終止確認的會計政
            have any impact on the interim condensed consolidated                        策與該等修訂本一致 ， 且本集團
            financial information. The Group will provide additional                     並無該等修訂本所規範的金融資

  3   OPERATING SEGMENT INFORMATION                                           3   經營分部資料

      The Group is principally engaged in the provision of a variety              本集團主要從事提供各種醫療健康業
      of healthcare businesses, including the provision of healthcare             務 ， 包括提供醫療健康服務和銷售醫
      services and sale of healthcare products.                                   療健康產品 。


      HKFRS 8 Operating Segments requires operating segments to                   香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第8號 經 營 分 部 規
      be identified on the basis of internal reporting about components           定 ， 經營分部按主要經營決策者為分
      of the Group that are regularly reviewed by the chief operating             配資源予各分部及評估其表現而定期
      decision-maker in order to allocate resources to segments and               審閱的有關本集團組成部分的內部報
      to assess their performance. The information reported to the                告為基礎而區分 。 為進行資源分配及
      directors of the Company, who are the chief operating decision-             表 現 評 估 而 向 本 公 司 董 事（主 要 經 營
      makers, for the purpose of resource allocation and assessment of            決 策 者）報 告 的 資 料 並 不 包 含 不 連 續
      performance does not contain discrete operating segment financial           的經營分部財務資料 ， 且董事審閱本
      information and the directors reviewed the financial results of the         集團整體的財務業績 。 因此 ， 並無呈
      Group as a whole. Therefore, no further information about the               報有關經營分部的進一步資料 。
      operating segment is presented.


      Seasonality of operations                                                   營運季節性

      In line with the healthcare industry in the Chinese mainland, where         本集團大部分收入來自中國內地 ， 及
      the majority of the Group’s revenue is derived from, the Group              與中國內地的醫療健康行業一樣 ， 本
      typically generate a majority of its total revenue in the second half       集團的大部分總收入通常來自下半年 ，
      of the year, during which customers in certain geographic regions           因為在下半年 ， 某些地區的客戶傾向
      tend to receive healthcare solutions and purchase healthcare                於接受醫療健康解決方案及購買醫療
      products to promote well-being with the weather getting cold.               健康產品 ， 以便在天氣變冷時促進健
      Additionally, customers usually avoid visiting medical institutions         康 。 此外 ， 客戶通常會在春節前後避
      shortly before and after the Chinese New Year. As a result of the           免前往醫療機構 。 因此 ， 上半年的收
      foregoing, the revenue was slightly lower in the first half of the          入略低 。
      year.

  8   INCOME TAX EXPENSES                                                     8   所得稅開支

      The Group is subject to income tax on an entity basis on profits            本集團須就本集團成員公司於所處及
      arising in or derived from the jurisdictions in which members of the        經營所在的司法權區產生或賺取的溢
      Group are domiciled and operate.                                            利 ， 按實體基準繳納所得稅 。


      Chinese mainland                                                            中國內地

      Pursuant to the PRC Income Tax Law and the respective                       根據中國所得稅法及相關規定 ， 於中
      regulations, a subsidiary which operates in Chinese mainland is             國內地經營的附屬公司須按應課稅收
      subject to corporate income tax at a rate of 25% on the taxable             入 的25% 繳 納 企 業 所 得 稅 。 於 報 告 期
      income. Certain subsidiaries of the Group in Chinese mainland are           內 ， 本集團的部分中國內地附屬公司
      qualified as “small and micro enterprises” and, accordingly, were           合 資 格 為「小 微 企 業」， 因 此 享 受 按
      entitled to a preferential income tax rate of 5% during the reporting       5% 的 優 惠 所 得 稅 稅 率 繳 稅 。 本 集 團
      period. Guangdong Gushengtang Health Technology Co., Limited                廣東固生堂中醫養生健康科技股份有
      of the Group is qualified as high technology enterprises and                限公司具備高科技企業資格 ， 因此可
      was hence granted a preferential CIT rate of 15% for the three              於2025年 至2028年 三 年 內 獲 得15% 的
      years from 2025 to 2028. Guangdong Jiyuan Traditional Chinese               優惠企業所得稅稅率 。 廣東濟元中醫
      Medicine Technology Co., Ltd. is entitled to an enterprise income           科 技 有 限 責 任 公 司 (Guangdong Jiyuan
      tax rate of 15% as part of the pilot preferential policy in the             Traditional Chinese Medicine Technology
      Nansha District of Guangzhou City.                                          Co., Ltd.) 根 據 廣 州 市 南 沙 區 的 試 點 優
                                                                                  惠政策 ， 享有15%的企業所得稅稅率 。


      Hong Kong                                                                   香港

      No provision for Hong Kong profits tax has been made as the                 由於本公司於報告期內並無源自香港
      Company had no assessable profits derived from or earned                    或在香港賺取的應課稅溢利 ， 故並無
      in Hong Kong during the reporting period. The subsidiary                    就香港利得稅作出撥備 。 在香港註冊
      incorporated in Hong Kong is subject to income tax at the rate of           成立的附屬公司須就期內在香港產生
      16.5% on the estimated assessable profits arising in Hong Kong              的 估 計 應 課 稅 溢 利 按16.5% 稅 率 繳 納 所
      during the periods.                                                         得稅 。
